    "type": "skill_listing",
    "content": "- update-config: Use this skill to configure the Claude Code harness via settings.json. Automated behaviors (\"from now on when X\", \"each time X\", \"whenever X\", \"before/after X\") require hooks configured in settings.json - the harness executes these, not Claude, so memory/preferences cannot fulfill them. Also use for: permissions (\"allow X\", \"add permission\", \"move permission to\"), env vars (\"set X=Y\"), hook troubleshooting, or any changes to settings.json/settings.local.json files. Examples: \"allow npm commands\", \"add bq permission to global settings\", \"move permission to user settings\", \"set DEBUG=true\", \"when claude stops show X\". For simple settings like theme/model, suggest the /config command.\n- keybindings-help: Use when the user wants to customize keyboard shortcuts, rebind keys, add chord bindings, or modify ~/.claude/keybindings.json. Examples: \"rebind ctrl+s\", \"add a chord shortcut\", \"change the submit key\", \"customize keybindings\".\n- simplify: Review changed code for reuse, quality, and efficiency, then fix any issues found.\n- fewer-permission-prompts: Scan your transcripts for common read-only Bash and MCP tool calls, then add a prioritized allowlist to project .claude/settings.json to reduce permission prompts.\n- loop: Run a prompt or slash command on a recurring interval (e.g. /loop 5m /foo). Omit the interval to let the model self-pace. - When the user wants to set up a recurring task, poll for status, or run something repeatedly on an interval (e.g. \"check the deploy every 5 minutes\", \"keep running /babysit-prs\"). Do NOT invoke for one-off tasks.\n- schedule: Create, update, list, or run scheduled remote agents (routines) that execute on a cron schedule. - When the user wants to schedule a recurring remote agent, set up automated tasks, create a cron job for Claude Code, or manage their scheduled agents/routines. Also use when the user wants a one-time scheduled run (\"run this once at 3pm\", \"remind me to check X tomorrow\").\n- claude-api: Build, debug, and optimize Claude API / Anthropic SDK apps. Apps built with this skill should include prompt caching. Also handles migrating existing Claude API code between Claude model versions (4.5 → 4.6, 4.6 → 4.7, retired-model replacements).\nTRIGGER when: code imports `anthropic`/`@anthropic-ai/sdk`; user asks for the Claude API, Anthropic SDK, or Managed Agents; user adds/modifies/tunes a Claude feature (caching, thinking, compaction, tool use, batch, files, citations, memory) or model (Opus/Sonnet/Haiku) in a file; questions about prompt caching / cache hit rate in an Anthropic SDK project.\nSKIP: file imports `openai`/other-provider SDK, filename like `*-openai.py`/`*-generic.py`, provider-neutral code, general programming/ML.\n- init: Initialize a new CLAUDE.md file with codebase documentation\n- review: Review a pull request\n- security-review: Complete a security review of the pending changes on the current branch",

PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\\\\\n\\\\\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. ACL Rolling Review format (acl2023.sty).\\\"\\n  page_limit: 8\\n  deadline: 2026-05-25\\n  core_claim: >\\n    ISR (Intensional Structural Reward), derived via Solver Trace Ablation,\\n    serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers\\n    but fails as a training signal due to meta-level Goodhart's Law — the diagnostic\\n    metric becomes gamed when optimized, with apparent improvements explained by\\n    premise minimization artifacts.\\n  created_at: 2026-05-14T18:00:00Z\\n  updated_at: 2026-05-14T19:05:00Z\\n  update_note: \\\"Director approved with 5 corrections applied: taxonomy 6→7, Claim 3 Prolog STA 3/3 gaming complete, conclusion limitations updated, tab5/fig4 expanded, registry_refs added.\\\"\\n\\npaper_outline:\\n  - section: abstract\\n    word_budget: 250\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Problem: GRPO formalizers achieve high prove rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs; no diagnostic exists\\\"\\n      - \\\"Method: STA derives ISR via leave-one-out component necessity testing on solver inputs\\\"\\n      - \\\"Claim 1: ISR detects hacking invisible to prove_rate (0.64→0.13→0.003 collapse over 100→500→1000 steps)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Claim 2: ISR-as-reward exhibits meta-level Goodhart — β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming (H7=64.5% vs 0% random), premise-matched 1.06x; β=0.3 PR collapses (18% vs 48% random)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Claim 3: ISR diagnostic transfers to Prolog (cross-logic generality)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Taxonomy: 7 distinct hacking modes identified across conditions (including query-as-fact gaming discovered in Prolog STA)\\\"\\n    dependencies: [experiments, method, discussion]\\n    tables: []\\n    figures: []\\n    registry_refs: []\\n\\n  - section: introduction\\n    word_budget: 1200\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Hook: A GRPO formalizer achieves 93% prove rate — but 87% of its proved outputs embed the conclusion in the premises. Structural shortcuts invisible to execution reward.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Gap: Execution-only evaluation (prove_rate) conflates genuine reasoning with degenerate shortcuts. No principled diagnostic exists for structural integrity in neurosymbolic GRPO.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Insight: Component necessity testing (LOO ablation on solver inputs) yields ISR — AND when this diagnostic becomes reward, it exhibits meta-level Goodhart: the model games the metric itself.\\\"\\n      - \\\"Mechanism: STA protocol + systematic study across 6 reward conditions × 3 seeds × up to 1000 steps\\\"\\n      - \\\"Contributions: (C1) ISR as diagnostic with complete collapse trajectory; (C2) Meta-level Goodhart empirical characterization with premise-count confound analysis; (C3) Cross-logic diagnostic transfer; (C4) 7-mode hacking taxonomy\\\"\\n    dependencies: [experiments]\\n    citation_hints:\\n      - \\\"GRPO: deepseekmath\\\"\\n      - \\\"Neurosymbolic: satlm, logic-lm, linc\\\"\\n      - \\\"Reward hacking: gao-reward-overoptimization, karwowski-goodhart-rl\\\"\\n      - \\\"Goodhart taxonomy: manheim-goodhart-taxonomy\\\"\\n    figures: [fig1_sta_overview]\\n    tables: []\\n    registry_refs: []\\n\\n  - section: related_work\\n    word_budget: 900\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"Cluster 1 — Neurosymbolic Reasoning with LLMs: Logic-LM, LINC, SatLM, LoRP, FoVer pipeline approach (prompting); Mellgren Prolog GRPO (RL). STA differs: structural reward + diagnostic focus\\\"\\n      - \\\"Cluster 2 — Reward Shaping & Process Supervision: Lightman verify-step, Math-Shepherd, RLSF symbolic feedback, Rubric-GRPO. STA's ISR is solver-grounded process signal; our finding: process reward can itself Goodhart\\\"\\n      - \\\"Cluster 3 — Reward Hacking & Goodhart's Law: Gao scaling laws, Karwowski geometric, Manheim taxonomy, Skalse characterization, Pan misspecification. We contribute empirical Goodhart in formal reasoning + meta-level (proxy games itself)\\\"\\n      - \\\"Cluster 4 — Mutation Testing & Component Analysis: DeMillo 1978, Jia-Harman survey, Just mutants. STA adapts LOO mutation from SE to formal logic\\\"\\n    dependencies: [introduction]\\n    citation_hints:\\n      - \\\"Must cite: deepseekmath, logic-lm, linc, satlm, mellgren-prolog-grpo\\\"\\n      - \\\"Must cite: lightman-verify-step, gao-reward-overoptimization, karwowski-goodhart-rl, manheim-goodhart-taxonomy\\\"\\n      - \\\"Must cite: demillo1978, folio, proofwriter\\\"\\n    figures: []\\n    tables: []\\n    registry_refs: []\\n\\n  - section: method\\n    word_budget: 1200\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"3.1 Task: NL → Formal Logic formalization, verified by external solver (Prover9 for FOL, SWI-Prolog for Prolog)\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.2 GRPO Training: execution-only reward r_exec ∈ {0,1}, group relative policy optimization\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.3 Solver Trace Ablation (STA): LOO ablation protocol — for proved sample with n components, remove each component, re-run solver, ISR = fraction of components whose removal causes proof failure\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.4 ISR Definition: ISR(x) = (1/n) Σ_i 1[solver fails on x\\\\\\\\{c_i}], bounded [0,1]. ISR=1 → all necessary; ISR=0 → none necessary\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.5 Reward Formulations: exec-only, additive ISR (r_exec·(1+β·ISR)), additive random control, convex combination, SFT baseline\\\"\\n      - \\\"3.6 Hacking Taxonomy: 7 modes — H1 (multi-premise conclusion embedding), H7 (single premise = conclusion), tautological collapse, repetition collapse, complexity-without-utility, contradiction explosion, query-as-fact gaming (Prolog STA s44, bypasses text-match classifier)\\\"\\n    dependencies: []\\n    notation_table:\\n      r_exec: \\\"binary execution reward (solver success)\\\"\\n      ISR: \\\"Intensional Structural Reward\\\"\\n      beta: \\\"ISR reward weight\\\"\\n      n: \\\"number of components in formalization\\\"\\n      c_i: \\\"i-th component (premise/rule)\\\"\\n      H1: \\\"multi-premise conclusion embedding\\\"\\n      H7: \\\"single premise = conclusion\\\"\\n    figures: [fig1_sta_overview]\\n    tables: []\\n    registry_refs: [sta-phase0-baseline, plan003-eval-exec-only-r5]\\n\\n  - section: experiments\\n    word_budget: 2800\\n    core_bullets:\\n      - \\\"4.1 Setup: Qwen3-4B, GRPO (K=8, 500 steps default), ProofWriter-FOL-100 eval, Prover9/SWI-Prolog solvers, 3 seeds (42/43/44)\\\"\\n      - \\\"4.2 ISR as Diagnostic (Claim 1): ISR collapse trajectory 0.6423→0.1324→0.0027 over 100→500→1000 steps (Fig2 with embedded data); 3	description: "当你准备向 Director 汇报前、收到 cron/worker 回调、审批通过准备开跑、怀疑 Worker 池不够饱和时,立即使用本 skill。四部分:A) GPU 分配率(分配了没跑=失职) B) GPU 效率 C) 非 GPU 任务并行(Worker 池<2=失职) D) 汇报必须带 [资源] 行。"
4	always: false
5	roles:
6	  - director
7	  - agent
8	---
9	
10	# 资源饱和度自检（硬约束）
11	
12	## A. GPU 分配率检查
13	
14	调 `get_project_resource_usage(project)` 看:
15	- 已分配给本项目的 GPU 总数和具体卡号
16	- 实际在跑任务的 GPU 数和具体卡号（交叉 registry RUNNING + nvidia-smi 进程）
17	- 分配了但没跑的 GPU 清单 ⚠️
18	
19	任何"分配了但没跑"的 GPU = 失职。必须立即:
20	- 派 Worker 铺 dry-run / 下一批实验代码准备
21	- 铺 ablation（从 experiment-matrix 空白格子里挑）
22	- 确实无实验可铺 → 向 Director 汇报"暂无可铺实验"+ 建议下一步方向(扩 ablation 维度 / 加 baseline / 加 benchmark)。**不要建议归还 GPU**——项目级 GPU 归还只在归档时由 `archive_project` 自动触发, 不是 Director 中途的动作
23	
24	**单实验不占多卡**: 分配 4 卡但实验只用 1 卡 → 另 3 卡必须并行跑其他实验,不能空占。
25	
26	## B. GPU 效率检查
27	
28	调 `get_gpu_health(server)`:
29	- 空闲 GPU（util < 10% 持续 > 5 min）→ 立即铺任务
30	- 低利用率（util < 50% 持续 > 15 min）→ spawn_worker 诊断（batch / IO / 同步）
31	- OOM 风险（显存 > 90% 持续 10 min）→ 主动降 batch / 调优
32	
33	## C. 非 GPU 任务并行
34	
35	GPU 在跑 ≠ 你空闲。以下 CPU / 网络任务**必须**派独立 Worker 并行:
36	- 下一批实验的数据集下载 / 预处理 / fingerprint
37	- 下一批实验的代码准备 / dry-run / 环境配置
38	- 论文文献调研 / related work 检索
39	- 已完成实验的 log 深度分析 / metric 聚合
40	- 论文章节起草
41	- 代码 review / refactor
42	
43	**硬约束**: 任一时刻 Worker 池 < 2 个 = 失职（除非真的全部下游阻塞在等 GPU,这时应当向 Director 申请扩 GPU）。
44	
45	## D. 汇报格式
46	
47	向 Director 汇报时开头必须带资源声明行:
48	
49	    [资源] GPU 分配 N / 实际在跑 M | Worker 池 K (其中 GPU 任务 A / CPU 任务 B)
50	
```

</details>

`fixed`: `beta_t = beta`\\n  - `linear`: `beta_t = beta * min(step / max_steps, 1.0)` — 0 到 beta 线性增长\\n  - `sigmoid`: `beta_t = beta / (1 + exp(-(step - midpoint) / (T/10)))` — S 曲线\\n- **step 同步**（L319）：`LocalLogCallback.on_step_end` 将 `state.global_step` 写入 `schedule_state['current_step']`\\n- **beta_t 记录**（L284-285, L338, L349）：每步的 `beta_t` 写入 `reward_log.jsonl` 和日志\\n\\n### 2. 训练命令（copy-paste ready）\\n\\n**Run 1 — Linear ramp**:\\n```bash\\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0\\ncd /root/solver-trace-ablation\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\npython3 scripts/train_grpo.py \\\\\\n  --config configs/grpo_additive_bonus.yaml \\\\\\n  --data_path data/train_500_seed42.jsonl \\\\\\n  --num_steps 500 --seed 42 --beta 0.1 \\\\\\n  --reward_mode additive_bonus \\\\\\n  --beta_schedule linear \\\\\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan015_linear_s42\\n```\\n\\n**Run 2 — Sigmoid**:\\n```bash\\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0\\ncd /root/solver-trace-ablation\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\npython3 scripts/train_grpo.py \\\\\\n  --config configs/grpo_additive_bonus.yaml \\\\\\n  --data_path data/train_500_seed42.jsonl \\\\\\n  --num_steps 500 --seed 42 --beta 0.1 \\\\\\n  --reward_mode additive_bonus \\\\\\n  --beta_schedule sigmoid --beta_schedule_midpoint 250 \\\\\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan015_sigmoid_s42\\n```\\n\\n注意：按 C002 约束，`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` 使用 `export` 而非 inline 赋值。\\n\\n### 3. 注意事项\\n\\n- 两个 run 不能同时跑在同一张 GPU 上（按 C007，提交前需 `nvidia-smi` 确认 GPU 空闲）\\n- `--config configs/grpo_additive_bonus.yaml` 中的默认值可能覆盖 CLI 的 `--beta 0.1`，需确认 YAML 中 beta 字段的值（config merge 逻辑是 YAML 仅填充 CLI 未设置的值，CLI 显式设置的 `--beta 0.1` 会保留）\\n- 实际上 config merge 代码（L389-394）只在 `current is None` 时覆盖，`--beta` 默认值是 0.3 不是 None，所以 CLI 传的 `0.1` 会被保留——没问题\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

`fixed`: `beta_t = beta`\\n  - `linear`: `beta_t = beta * min(step / max_steps, 1.0)` — 0 到 beta 线性增长\\n  - `sigmoid`: `beta_t = beta / (1 + exp(-(step - midpoint) / (T/10)))` — S 曲线\\n- **step 同步**（L319）：`LocalLogCallback.on_step_end` 将 `state.global_step` 写入 `schedule_state['current_step']`\\n- **beta_t 记录**（L284-285, L338, L349）：每步的 `beta_t` 写入 `reward_log.jsonl` 和日志\\n\\n### 2. 训练命令（copy-paste ready）\\n\\n**Run 1 — Linear ramp**:\\n```bash\\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0\\ncd /root/solver-trace-ablation\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\npython3 scripts/train_grpo.py \\\\\\n  --config configs/grpo_additive_bonus.yaml \\\\\\n  --data_path data/train_500_seed42.jsonl \\\\\\n  --num_steps 500 --seed 42 --beta 0.1 \\\\\\n  --reward_mode additive_bonus \\\\\\n  --beta_schedule linear \\\\\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan015_linear_s42\\n```\\n\\n**Run 2 — Sigmoid**:\\n```bash\\nexport CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=0\\ncd /root/solver-trace-ablation\\nsource /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base\\npython3 scripts/train_grpo.py \\\\\\n  --config configs/grpo_additive_bonus.yaml \\\\\\n  --data_path data/train_500_seed42.jsonl \\\\\\n  --num_steps 500 --seed 42 --beta 0.1 \\\\\\n  --reward_mode additive_bonus \\\\\\n  --beta_schedule sigmoid --beta_schedule_midpoint 250 \\\\\\n  --output_dir /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/plan015_sigmoid_s42\\n```\\n\\n注意：按 C002 约束，`CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES` 使用 `export` 而非 inline 赋值。\\n\\n### 3. 注意事项\\n\\n- 两个 run 不能同时跑在同一张 GPU 上（按 C007，提交前需 `nvidia-smi` 确认 GPU 空闲）\\n- `--config configs/grpo_additive_bonus.yaml` 中的默认值可能覆盖 CLI 的 `--beta 0.1`，需确认 YAML 中 beta 字段的值（config merge 逻辑是 YAML 仅填充 CLI 未设置的值，CLI 显式设置的 `--beta 0.1` 会保留）\\n- 实际上 config merge 代码（L389-394）只在 `current is None` 时覆盖，`--beta` 默认值是 0.3 不是 None，所以 CLI 传的 `0.1` 会被保留——没问题\"}}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"

1	\section{Experiments}
2	\label{sec:experiments}
3	
4	We evaluate STA along three axes: as a diagnostic for reward hacking (\S\ref{sec:diagnostic}); as a training reward exhibiting meta-level Goodhart's Law (\S\ref{sec:goodhart}); and as a cross-logic protocol (\S\ref{sec:crosslogic}). We synthesize results into a hacking taxonomy (\S\ref{sec:taxonomy}).
5	
6	\subsection{Setup}\label{sec:setup}
7	
8	We train Qwen3-4B with GRPO~\citep{deepseekmath} ($K{=}8$ rollouts, 500 steps default, 1000 where noted) across seeds $\{42,43,44\}$ on a fixed 500-example FOLIO~\citep{folio} subset held constant across conditions. %% data: C001
9	Evaluation uses 100 held-out examples with greedy decoding; cross-run ISR values are computed uniformly by \texttt{eval\_sta.py}. %% C004
10	We report PR, proved-only ISR (ISR$^*$), hacking rates (H7, H1\footnote{H1/H7 numbering follows our taxonomy (Appendix~\ref{app:hacking-modes}).}), and non-hacking rate.\footnote{Non-hacking: not matching any of seven identified patterns (Table~\ref{tab:hacking-taxonomy-app}); does not imply semantic correctness.}
11	Full hyperparameters in Appendix~\ref{app:setup}.
12	
13	\subsection{ISR as Diagnostic}
14	\label{sec:diagnostic}
15	
16	\begin{figure}[t]
17	\centering
18	\includegraphics[width=0.95\columnwidth]{figures/fig2_isr_collapse.pdf}
19	\caption{ISR collapse under exec-only GRPO (seed~42). PR rises 32\%$\to$96\% while proved-only ISR falls 0.642$\to$0.003 over 1000 steps. By step 1000, 94.8\% of proved samples have ISR${=}0$ and mean component count inflates 6.58$\to$18.55 (contradiction explosion).}
20	\label{fig:isr-collapse}
21	\end{figure}
22	
23	\textbf{Collapse trajectory.}
24	Figure~\ref{fig:isr-collapse} traces exec-only GRPO (seed~42): PR rises 32\%$\to$93\%$\to$96\% while ISR$^*$ falls $0.642{\to}0.132{\to}0.003$ over 1000 steps. %% registry: plan003-eval-exec-only-r5, plan001-exec-only-s42-r2, plan016-extended-1000-s42
25	By step 500, 87.1\% of proved samples embed the conclusion (H1); by 1000, H1${=}100$\% with mean 18.55 components.
26	The monotonic ISR$^*$ decline shows extended training worsens structural degeneration invisible to PR.
27	
28	\textbf{Cross-seed instability.}
29	The three exec-only seeds diverge radically: s42 hacks via H1 (PR${=}93$\%, H1${=}87.1$\%); s43 collapses to PR${=}13$\% via repetition (87\% hit max length); s44 reaches PR${=}98$\% via tautological collapse (H7${=}100$\%). %% registry: plan001-exec-only-s42-r2, plan001-exec-only-s43-r4, plan001-exec-only-s44
30	Despite PR$\in[13,98]$\%, all three exhibit structural degeneration detectable by ISR; the $\sigma{=}\pm47$\,pp cross-seed variance shows aggregate metrics obscure qualitatively different failure modes (per-seed: Table~\ref{tab:beta01-3seed}).
31	
32	\textbf{SFT baseline.}
33	SFT achieves PR${=}14$\% with ISR$^*{=}0.571$: 92.9\% non-hacking, only 1/14 exhibits H1, zero H7. %% registry: plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4
34	The near-absence of hacking is consistent with hacking being RL-specific; a 100-step RL checkpoint analysis controlling for the PR-level confound is in Appendix~\ref{app:100step}.
35	
36	\subsection{Meta-Level Goodhart's Law}
37	\label{sec:goodhart}
38	
39	ISR-as-reward provides the first empirical demonstration of Goodhart's Law at the meta level~\citep{manheim-goodhart-taxonomy}: the diagnostic itself becomes the target of gaming when optimized.
40	
41	\begin{table}[t]
42	\centering
43	\small
44	\resizebox{\columnwidth}{!}{%
45	\begin{tabular}{lcccccc}
46	\toprule
47	\textbf{Condition} & $\boldsymbol{\beta}$ & \textbf{PR\%}$\uparrow$ & \textbf{ISR}$^*$ & \textbf{H7\%} & \textbf{H1\%} & \textbf{N-H\%} \\
48	\midrule
49	Base (0-shot) & --- & 12 & --- & --- & --- & --- \\ %% registry: sta-phase0-baseline
50	SFT & --- & 14 & 0.571 & 0 & 7 & \textbf{93} \\ %% registry: plan009-sft-baseline-s42-r4

  "task_prompt": "## 任务: westd-39279 磁盘清理\n\n**目标**: 将数据盘 `/root/autodl-tmp/` 使用率从 96% 降至 <85%。需要释放至少 30GB。\n\n**服务器**: westd-39279\n**环境**: conda base（`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`）\n\n### 清理策略（按优先级）\n\n1. **清理已完成实验的 checkpoint**（最大收益）:\n   - `ls -lhS /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/*/checkpoint-*/` 找到最大的 checkpoint 目录\n   - 保留 RUNNING 实验的 checkpoint（exp1-random-b03-s44-r2，PID 51297 正在跑，**不要动它的目录**）\n   - 已 DONE 实验（所有 plan001/plan002/plan005/plan006/plan009/plan013/plan016/plan017/plan018/exp1-*-s43/exp1-isr-b03-s44-r2/exp4-100step-h1h7/exp2-exec-s43-greedy）的 checkpoint 可以安全删除\n   - **注意**: 先 `du -sh` 确认大小，再删除\n\n2. **清理 optimizer.pt**:\n   - `find /root/autodl-tmp/outputs/ -name \"optimizer.pt\" -size +1G` \n   - 这些文件 ~15GB 每个，eval 不需要\n\n3. **清理 wandb 缓存**:\n   - `du -sh /root/autodl-tmp/.wandb_cache/ /root/.cache/wandb/ /root/wandb/` \n   - 如果大就删\n\n4. **清理 HF 缓存中的临时文件**:\n   - `du -sh /root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache/` 看大小\n   - 不要删模型本身（Qwen3-4B 需要），只删 `.locks` 和 `blobs` 中的重复\n\n5. **清理旧日志**:\n   - `find /root/runs/ -name \"*.log\" -size +100M`\n   - `find /root/solver-trace-ablation/outputs/ -name \"*.json\" -size +50M`\n\n### 安全约束\n- **绝对不要删除** `/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/` 下 exp1-random-b03-s44-r2 相关的目录（正在训练中）\n- **不要删除** `/root/autodl-tmp/models/Qwen3-4B/`（需要的模型）\n- **不要删除** `/root/solver-trace-ablation/` 下的代码和数据文件\n- 每删一个目录前先 `du -sh` 确认大小并记录\n\n### 产出\n完成后报告: 清理前后 `df -h /root/autodl-tmp/`，释放了多少空间，删了什么。",
  "tool_groups": [

  ]
}

{
  "task_type": "review:paper-consistency-v2",
  "project": "solver-trace-ablation",
  "task_prompt": "## 任务: 论文一致性审查 + 质量评估\n\n**目标**: 审查 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/` 下的论文草稿，检查数据一致性、写作质量、页数合规。\n\n### 检查项\n\n1. **数字一致性**: 论文中引用的所有指标（PR、ISR、H7 rate、genuine rate 等）是否与 registry 数据一致。用 `read_registry` 获取实际指标并逐一对照论文中的数字。重点检查:\n   - experiments.tex 中的所有百分比数字\n   - 表格中的数据\n   - abstract 和 introduction 中引用的关键数字\n\n2. **Claims vs Evidence 对齐**:\n   - Claim 1 (ISR 诊断): 0.642→0.132→0.003 collapse trajectory 有 registry 支撑？\n   - Claim 2 (Meta-level Goodhart): premise-count-matched 1.06x, H7=64.5%/0% 有 registry 支撑？\n   - Claim 3 (跨逻辑): Prolog 3/3 gaming, 0% genuine 有 registry 支撑？\n\n3. **引用完整性**: references.bib 中的引用是否都在正文中出现？是否有未定义的 \\cite 引用？\n\n4. **页数**: 主体内容是否在 8 页以内（ACL format）？检查 main.pdf 的页数分布。\n\n5. **写作质量评估**: 每个 section 打分 (1-5)，标注具体需要改进的段落。关注:\n   - 是否有 cherry-picking（禁止）\n   - 强度词是否匹配证据（\"significantly\" 需统计检验）\n   - 论点逻辑是否通顺\n   - 是否有重复内容\n\n6. **D063 影响评估**: exp1 系列使用了错误测试集 (test_100.jsonl vs proofwriter_fol_100.jsonl)。检查论文是否引用了任何 exp1 系列的数据。如果引用了，标记为 ⚠️ 需要修正。\n\n### 产出\n生成一份审查报告，按 section 列出问题清单 + 严重程度（critical/major/minor）。报告写入 `save_project_file(file=\"paper_review_loop10.md\")`。\n\n### 注意\n- 所有论文文件在 `/home/ubuntu/.agent-ml-research-idea_gen_0509_3/projects/solver-trace-ablation/paper/` 目录\n- Registry 数据用 `read_registry(project=\"solver-trace-ablation\", fields=[\"*\"])` 获取\n- 论文 writing_plan.yaml 在项目文件中: `read_project_file(project=\"solver-trace-ablation\", file=\"writing_plan.yaml\")`",
